--- PAGE 4 ---
PREFACE

This is the first edition of the Constitution of India in Dogri language. In this
edition the text of the Constitution has been updated by incorporating therein all
the Amendments upto and including the Constitution (One Hundred and Fifth
Amendment) Act, 2021

The present edition of the Constitution of India is being published by the
Official Languages Wing (Regional Unit), Legislative Department, Ministry of
Law and Justice, New Delhi, as part of the implementation of the decision of the
Central Government to make available the Constitution of India and other Central
Laws to the general public in their own regional language to enable them to
understand the law of the land in its true spirit to fulfill the constitutional mandate.

This edition is being brought out by the Government of India with the
sincere efforts and cooperation of University of Jammu and translated into Dogri
language by Prof. (Dr.) Archana Kesar, Department of Dogri, University of
Jammu, Sh. Prakash Premi, Dogri writer and translator, Sh. Yash Paul Nirmal,
Dogri writer and translator and Smt. Nirmal Vikram Dogri writer and translator
under the guidance of Legal Expert Prof. (Dr.) Arvind Jasrotia, Dean Faculty of
Law, University of Jammu.
New Delhi Dr. Reeta Vasishta
March, 2022 Secretary of the Government of India.
